Across the tested operating points, the best RLIM-SBRNN receiver beats the best uncoded receiver, chosen between threshold and SBRNN detection, in $46$ of $59$ cases, with a mean gain of $10.36\times$ over those wins. We also propose an RLIM-tailored training mask for compact SBRNN detectors, improving the unmasked RLIM-SBRNN in $227$ of $236$ comparisons with $3.267\times$ mean gain when masking is beneficial. Finally, the compact masked RLIM-SBRNN is competitive with channel-state-aware MLSE despite using no channel knowledge.

</details>


### [145] [The Capacity Region for Classes of Sum-Broadcast Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12839)
*Amin Gohari,Yi Liu,Chandra Nair*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了一类由多个广播信道相加构成的信道模型的容量区域，证明辅助接收机外界与 Marton 内界一致，并推广了 El Gamal 关于两个反向退化广播信道之和的经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播信道容量区域通常难以精确刻画；已有结果只覆盖较特殊的反向退化广播信道组合。本文希望统一处理由退化、less-noisy、more-capable、确定性或半确定性等组件组成的广播信道之和，并进一步扩展到所谓 primary broadcast channels。

Method: 作者使用此前提出的 auxiliary-receiver outer bound，并证明在目标信道类别下该外界与 Marton inner bound 完全匹配，从而得到精确容量区域；同时定义 primary broadcast channels，并对其信道和建立类似的内外界匹配结论。

Result: 论文得到这些广播信道之和的容量区域；该结论严格推广了 El Gamal 1980 年关于两个反向退化广播信道之和的已知容量结果，并覆盖更广泛的信道组件类别。

Conclusion: 通过证明辅助接收机外界与 Marton 内界一致，本文为多类广播信道组合提供了统一的容量刻画框架，显示 Marton 编码在这些结构化信道和模型中是最优的。

Abstract: We compute the capacity region of a sum of broadcast channels whose components are degraded, less-noisy, more-capable, deterministic, or semi-deterministic. We achieve this by showing that an auxiliary-receiver outer bound, previously introduced by some of the authors, matches Marton's inner bound. This result generalizes a previously known result for the sum of two reversely degraded broadcast channels due to El Gamal (1980). Moreover, we define a class of primary broadcast channels and show an analogous result for the sum of primary broadcast channels.

</details>


### [146] [JSCGC: Joint Source-Channel-Generation Coding for Wireless Generative Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12858)
*Tong Wu,Zhiyong Chen,Guo Lu,Li Song,Feng Yang,Meixia Tao,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出联合源信道生成编码（JSCGC），用接收端生成模型替代传统解码器，将通信重构问题转化为受信道条件控制的生成问题，以提升图像传输的感知与语义质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分离式编码与学习式JSCC多基于Shannon率失真理论，依赖通用失真度量；这些指标难以刻画复杂的人类视觉感知，导致重建图像模糊或不真实。

Method: 提出JSCGC生成式通信范式：接收信号作为条件变量控制生成模型采样，从学习到的条件分布中生成图像；目标由确定性失真最小化转为在感知约束下最大化互信息。进一步设计统一的联合训练与高效随机采样框架，并从学习与推理阶段进行理论分析。

Result: 在潜空间图像传输实验中，JSCGC在多种信道条件下持续提升特征级、语义级与分布级质量，并表现出区别于传统失真错误的错误模式，即主要体现为语义不一致。

Conclusion: JSCGC为图像通信提供了一种从“精确重建”转向“受控生成”的新范式，在感知质量和语义表达方面优于传统失真驱动方法，但也引入了语义一致性方面的新型可靠性问题。

Abstract: Conventional communication systems, including both separation-based coding and learning-based joint source-channel coding (JSCC), are typically designed under Shannon's rate-distortion theory. However, relying on generic distortion metrics fails to capture complex human visual perception, often resulting in blurred or unrealistic reconstructions. In this paper, we propose Joint Source-Channel-Generation Coding (JSCGC), a generative communication paradigm that replaces the conventional decoder with a generative model at the receiver. The received signal is treated as a condition that controls the sampling process into the learned conditional distribution, reformulating communication from deterministic reconstruction for distortion minimization to controlled generation for mutual information maximization under perceptual constraints. Based on this formulation, we develop a unified joint training and efficient stochastic sampling framework, and provide theoretical analysis of its effectiveness in both learning and inference stages. Extensive experiments on latent-space image transmission demonstrate that the JSCGC consistently improves feature-based, semantic-level, and distributional quality across diverse channel conditions, while exhibiting a distinct error behavior characterized by semantic inconsistency rather than distortion.

</details>


### [147] [Pinching-Antenna Enabled Multicell Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12888)
*Yunshu Chen,Qing Xue,Meng Hua,Bingpeng Zhou,Shaodan Ma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究多波导夹持天线（PA）多小区通信系统，通过联合优化预编码矩阵、波导功率分配与天线位置来最大化加权和速率，并提出基于分式规划、块坐标下降和粒子群优化的交替优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多小区无线系统中，小区间干扰使加权和速率最大化问题高度耦合且非凸；同时，PA系统可通过动态调整夹持单元位置重构传播环境，提供新的空间自由度，因此需要设计有效的联合资源与结构优化方法。

Method: 首先使用分式规划引入辅助变量，将信号项与干扰项解耦；随后采用块坐标下降迭代优化预编码矩阵和波导功率分配，得到闭式或半闭式更新；针对高维非凸的PA位置优化问题，引入粒子群优化进行高效搜索，以提升算法可扩展性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在多种系统配置下，所提方案显著优于平均功率分配、固定天线位置、传统MIMO以及大规模MIMO等基线方法。

Conclusion: PA系统在大规模多小区无线通信中具有较强潜力；通过联合优化预编码、功率分配和天线部署，可有效抑制干扰并提升系统加权和速率。

Abstract: Pinching antenna (PA) systems have recently emerged as a promising flexible-antenna technology, which can reconstruct the wireless propagation environment by dynamically adjusting the positions of pinching elements along dielectric waveguides, thereby providing new spatial degrees of freedom (DoFs) for enhancing wireless system performance. This paper investigates a multi-waveguide PA-based multi-cell communication system, focusing on the joint optimization of precoding matrices, waveguide power allocation, and antenna placement to maximize the weighted sum rate (WSR). In multi-cell scenarios, inter-cell interference typically leads to a highly coupled and nonconvex WSR maximization problem. To address this challenge, an efficient alternating optimization framework is adopted to optimize each variable in an iterative way. Specifically, fractional programming is first employed to reformulate the original problem by introducing auxiliary variables that decouple the signal and interference terms. Based on this reformulation, block coordinate descent is then applied to optimize the precoding matrices and power allocation, leading to closed-form or semi-closed-form updates. For the high-dimensional and nonconvex PA placement problem, particle swarm optimization (PSO) is utilized to perform an efficient search and improve scalability. Numerical results demonstrate that, under various system configurations, the proposed scheme significantly outperforms baseline methods, including average power allocation, fixed antenna placement, conventional multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO), and massive MIMO. These results highlight the strong potential of PA systems for large-scale multi-cell wireless communications.

</details>


### [148] [Bounds and Constructions of Maximum Toroidal Distance Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13008)
*Pengjie Zhong,Jinquan Luo,Yufeng Song*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究模 q 环面空间中的最大环面距离（MTD）码，给出多种上界并构造了若干达到最优的显式码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格基密码中编码消息与解密噪声均位于模 q 空间，因此需要研究适合该几何结构的环面距离及其最优纠错/区分码，即最大环面距离码。

Method: 作者结合几何方法与线性规划方法，推导最小环面距离的 Plotkin 型上界、局部球-Plotkin 上界和 Delsarte 线性规划上界，并基于低维构造、一般构造以及 E8 格构造显式码。

Result: 论文证明部分上界在若干情形下是紧的；在维数 ell=2 构造四点 MTD 码族，在 ell=4 给出一般构造和多个显式最优实例，在 ell=8 利用 E8 格构造 C=2mE8∩Z_q^8（q=4m）并证明其为 MTD 码；ell=16,q=4 时构造出最小环面距离为 3 的码。

Conclusion: 该工作为模 q 环面空间中的最优码提供了新的理论上界与显式构造，特别是在 ell=2,4,8 中给出明确的 MTD 最优构造，并显示格几何与线性规划是研究此类码的有效工具。

Abstract: In lattice-based cryptographic schemes, both encoded messages and accumulated decryption noise are represented in a modulo $q$ space. Therefore, it is natural to study toroidal distances and maximum toroidal distance (MTD) codes. In this paper, we derive some upper bounds for minimum toroidal distance of a code, including a Plotkin-type bound, a local ball--Plotkin bound, and a Delsarte linear programming bound. We also exhibit examples showing that these bounds are sharp in some cases. Moreover, we present several code constructions with good minimum distance, some of which are MTD codes. For $\ell=2$, we obtain a family of four-point MTD codes in $\mathbb Z_q^2$. For $\ell=4$, we propose a general code construction and exhibit several explicit instances for specific values of $q$, some of which are proven to be MTD codes. For $\ell=8$, using the $E_8$ lattice, we construct codes $C=2mE_8\cap \mathbb Z_q^8$, where $q=4m$ and show that they are MTD codes. These results give explicit optimal constructions of MTD codes for $\ell=2,4,8$. In the case $\ell=16$, we construct a code with minimum toroidal distance $3$ for $q=4$, while the known upper bound in this case is $2\sqrt{3}$. Our main tools are geometric and linear programming methods.

</details>


### [149] [Technical Supplement Report on Full-Duplex FBMC/QAM MIMO Systems: Transceiver Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13023)
*Sudhakar Rai,Prem Singh,Ekant Sharma,Aditya K. Jagannatham,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该报告研究FBMC/QAM多用户MISO系统的上下行完整处理链、端到端等效信道与干扰机制，并提出在线随机连续凸近似的遍历和速率优化方法；仿真显示其在残余载波频偏下相较CP-OFDM具有BER和网络频谱效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CP-OFDM在频偏和频谱泄漏方面存在局限，FBMC/OQAM虽具良好频谱特性但受Balian-Low定理与固有干扰影响。论文旨在评估FBMC/QAM在多用户MISO场景中的可行性、干扰特性和性能收益，并解决其资源/功率优化问题。

Method: 构建FBMC/QAM上下行信号处理模型，推导包含ICI、ISI、固有干扰和残余自干扰的端到端等效信道；对比CP-OFDM与FBMC/OQAM；分析PHYDYAS、Type-I、Type-II原型滤波器及MRT/ZF预编码下的干扰功率分解；提出在线随机连续凸近似框架进行遍历和速率最大化，并给出闭式功率更新。

Result: 仿真表明，在存在残余载波频偏时，FBMC/QAM相较CP-OFDM可获得更低误码率和更高网络频谱效率；不同原型滤波器和预编码方式对干扰组成与系统性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: FBMC/QAM在多用户MISO系统中兼具较好的频谱局部化与QAM兼容性，通过合理滤波器、预编码和在线功率优化，可在频偏鲁棒性和频谱效率方面优于CP-OFDM，并为实际宽带无线系统提供有竞争力的候选方案。

Abstract: This technical report presents the design and analysis of filter bank multicarrier (FBMC)/QAM multi-user MISO systems. We describe the complete uplink and downlink signal processing chains and characterize the end-to-end effective channel, including inter-carrier interference, inter-symbol interference, intrinsic interference, and residual self-interference. We compare FBMC/QAM with CP-OFDM and FBMC/OQAM through the lens of the Balian-Low theorem, and analyze prototype filter choices (PHYDYAS, Type-I, and Type-II), including the interference power breakdown under MRT and ZF precoding. Furthermore, we present an online stochastic successive convex approximation framework for ergodic sum-rate maximization with closed-form power updates, and contrast it with offline Monte Carlo-based approaches. Simulation results demonstrate the BER and network spectral efficiency advantages of FBMC/QAM over CP-OFDM under residual carrier frequency offset.

</details>


### [150] [Active Sensing-assisted UAV Communications with Jittering: Framework and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13036)
*Guangji Chen,Long Shi,Qingqing Wu,Qiaoyan Peng,Caihong Kai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出面向地面到无人机链路的两阶段主动感知辅助通信框架，以缓解无人机抖动导致的波束失准问题，并推导两类方案的AoA估计性能、可达速率和最优时间分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝网络支持无人机可靠通信时，无人机抖动会引起到达角变化和波束失准，从而显著降低通信质量；因此需要利用感知能力实时获取角度信息，以提升链路稳定性与速率。

Method: 设计两种两阶段方案：感知导向方案在第一阶段发送确定性信号用于无人机侧AoA估计，第二阶段利用估计AoA进行纯通信；通信导向方案在两个阶段均发送高斯承载信息信号，并基于随机高斯信号估计AoA。论文为两类方案构造最大似然AoA估计器，分析Cramér-Rao下界，推导可达速率闭式表达式，并给出最大化总体速率的半闭式最优第一阶段时间分配。

Result: 结果揭示了第一阶段时间分配在感知精度与通信速率之间的基本权衡；给出了通信导向方案优于感知导向方案的充分条件，呈现阈值结构；渐近分析表明在高发射功率下，两种方案相对于无抖动上界的性能损失趋近于零。

Conclusion: 主动感知辅助通信能够有效缓解无人机抖动引起的波束失准；通过合理选择信号设计和阶段时长，可在感知精度与通信效率之间取得最优折中，且高功率条件下可接近理想无抖动性能。

Abstract: Providing reliable communication for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) via existing cellular networks is crucial for enabling the rapid growth of the low-altitude economy. However, UAV jittering significantly degrades communication quality due to induced beam misalignment. Inspired by recent advances in integrated sensing and communication, we propose a novel two-stage active sensing-assisted communication framework tailored for ground-to-UAV links with jittering. Specifically, two schemes are conceived to leverage sensing for enhancing communication performance, namely the communication-oriented scheme and the sensing-oriented scheme. For the sensing-oriented scheme, deterministic signals are employed in the first stage to facilitate angle-of-arrival (AoA) acquisition at the UAV side, followed by pure communication service in the second stage by using the estimated AoA. In contrast, the communication-oriented scheme employs Gaussian information-bearing signals throughout both stages, with AoA estimation relying on Gaussian random signals. For both schemes, we provide maximum likelihood estimators for AoA, along with analytical results characterizing the Cramér-Rao bound. To capture the performance limit, closed-form expressions for the achievable rates of the two schemes are derived, unveiling a fundamental tradeoff between sensing and communication quality across the two stages by tuning the time allocated to the first stage. The optimal time allocation that maximizes the overall rate is obtained in semi-closed-form. Based on these results, we unveil a sufficient condition under which the communication-oriented scheme outperforms the sensing-oriented scheme, which admits an interesting threshold-based structure. Asymptotic analysis demonstrates that the performance loss of the proposed schemes relative to the jitter-free upper bound approaches zero in the high transmit power regime.

</details>


### [151] [Polar Decoding Tree Pruning Based on Soft Output Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13214)
*Li Shen,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于软输出可靠性估计的SCL极化码译码路径剪枝策略，在不损失纠错性能的前提下降低排序与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SCL译码会在列表中保留许多对最终译码输出贡献极小的候选路径，导致路径排序和计算开销较高。现有剪枝方法仍存在效率或性能折中问题，因此需要更准确地判断路径可靠性并动态删除低价值路径。

Method: 作者利用soft-output SCL与soft-output fast SCL译码中的块级软输出提取过程，近似估计每条译码路径为正确路径的概率；随后依据预设可靠性阈值，对低于阈值的路径进行剪枝。基于该思想构建SOP-SCL译码器及其快速版本SOP-FSCL译码器。

Result: 所提出方法显著降低译码复杂度，尤其减少无效路径带来的排序和计算负担；同时保持与原始SCL/FSCL相当的纠错性能，并优于已有先进剪枝极化码译码器。

Conclusion: 基于软输出概率估计的路径剪枝是一种有效的极化码SCL复杂度优化方案，可在性能无损的条件下提升译码效率，具有较好的实际实现价值。

Abstract: Although the successive cancellation list (SCL) decoding of polar codes exhibits excellent performance, it retains many decoding paths in the list with negligible contribution to the final output, resulting in high sorting and computational complexity. In this letter, we propose a novel pruning strategy to mitigate the decoding complexity. By leveraging the blockwise soft output extraction process of soft-output SCL and soft-output fast SCL decoding, we provide an accurate approximation of the probability that a decoding path is correct, and thus accordingly prune the paths failing to meet a pre-defined reliability threshold. The complexity reduction achieved by the proposed soft-output-based pruned SCL (SOP-SCL) decoder and its fast version, SOP-FSCL decoder, is significant, without any compromise in error-correction performance. Meanwhile, they also prove to be more efficient than state-of-the-art pruned polar decoders.

</details>


### [152] [A $q$-analogue of the rational normal curve and linearized Reed-Solomon codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13246)
*Valentina Astore,Martino Borello,Alain Couvreur,Flavio Salizzoni*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为线性化 Reed-Solomon 码建立了一个代数几何框架，通过引入有理正规曲线的 q-类比来刻画其结构，并分析相关坐标环的 Hilbert 函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamming 度量下线性码与射影代数簇之间已有深刻联系，但 sum-rank 度量下的线性化 Reed-Solomon 码虽在网络编码、分布式存储和后量子密码中重要，其结构、几何性质及区分器仍理解不足。

Method: 作者构造有理正规曲线的 q-模拟，将线性化 Reed-Solomon 码置于新的几何框架中；结合并推广 Hamming 与 rank-metric 场景中的 Schur 积技术到 sum-rank 度量；进一步研究相关点集的超曲面约束与坐标环 Hilbert 函数。

Result: 在特定参数选择下获得了线性化 Reed-Solomon 码的几何刻画；发现对应点集满足大量意外的 q+1 次超曲面条件；给出了相关坐标环 Hilbert 函数行为的详细描述，并确定其正则性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了线性化 Reed-Solomon 码背后的代数几何结构，扩展了经典码几何方法到 sum-rank 度量，并为理解 Gabidulin 码及构造潜在结构性区分器提供了新视角。

Abstract: The relationship between linear codes in the Hamming metric and projective algebraic varieties has led to deep interactions between coding theory and algebraic geometry, with classical examples such as Reed-Solomon codes and the rational normal curve. On the other hand, the sum-rank metric has recently gained attention due to applications in network coding, distributed storage, and post-quantum cryptography, with linearized Reed-Solomon codes emerging as optimal constructions. Despite recent advances, their structural and geometric properties are still not fully understood, and existing distinguishers remain limited. In this paper, we develop a geometric framework for linearized Reed-Solomon codes by considering a $q$-analogue of the rational normal curve. This yields a geometric characterization for certain parameter choices and reveals that the corresponding sets of points satisfy unexpectedly many $(q+1)$-degree hypersurface conditions. Our approach extends Schur-product-based techniques from the Hamming and rank-metric settings to the sum-rank metric case. Finally, we study the Hilbert function of the associated coordinate ring, providing a detailed description of its behavior and identifying its regularity, which also sheds new light on Gabidulin codes.

</details>


### [153] [Error Probability Analysis of Quantum Communication with Phase-squeezed M-PSK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13286)
*Nikos A. Mitsiou,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究相位压缩 M-PSK 在自适应 Mark-II 相位测量接收机下的符号错误概率，提出理论 POM 分析与高效近似 SEP 计算方法，显示相位压缩可显著提升光子效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M-PSK 的判决核心观测量是光学相位，而相位压缩态可降低相位噪声、改善通信可靠性；因此需要在物理可实现的相位测量接收机 Mark-II 下准确评估其 SEP 性能。

Method: 作者首先基于 Mark-II 方案在 Fock 基下的相位概率算子测度建立理论分析；随后提出两类 SEP 方法：一是推导压缩态噪声诱导的相位概率密度，并通过角向卷积纳入 Mark-II 测量不确定性；二是提出有效切向方差模型，将 SEP 写成含 Owen’s T 函数的闭式表达。

Result: 数值结果表明，相位压缩相比相干态传输显著降低 M-PSK 的 SEP，且星座阶数越高收益越大；在所研究场景中，随着平均发射光子数增加，相位压缩几乎可使 M-PSK 的光子效率翻倍。所提近似方法与 Mark-II POM 分析高度一致，通常误差约为 2–4 个光子。

Conclusion: 相位压缩量子态结合 Mark-II 自适应相位测量能够有效提升 M-PSK 通信性能；所提出的闭式与近似 SEP 工具兼具准确性和计算效率，适合用于相位压缩量子 M-PSK 系统分析。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the symbol error probability (SEP) of phase-squeezed M-ary phase-shift keying (M-PSK). Since the relevant observable for M-PSK detection is the optical phase, we adopt the adaptive Mark-II receiver which is a physically realizable phase measurement. First, we develop a theoretical analysis based on the phase probability operator measure (POM) of the Mark-II scheme in the Fock basis. Then, we develop two SEP methods based on the statistics of the received PSK symbol and the error introduced by the Mark-II measurement. The first method derives the phase probability density induced by the squeezed state noise and incorporates the additional Mark-II phase uncertainty through an angular convolution. Since this convolution does not admit a simple closed form, we also introduce an effective tangential-variance model, which yields a closed form SEP expression in terms of the Owen's T-function. Numerical results show that phase squeezing substantially reduces the SEP of M-PSK compared to coherent state transmission, with greater gains for higher constellation orders. Notably, for the investigated scenario, squeezing can almost double the photon efficiency of M-PSK as the mean number of transmitted photons increases. Finally, the proposed approximations closely follow the Mark-II POM analysis, typically within an accuracy of 2-4 photons, and therefore provide accurate and computationally efficient tools for analyzing phase squeezed quantum M-PSK communication.

</details>


### [154] [$W-δ-μ$ dual codes and LCD codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13467)
*Avanish Kumar Chaturvedi,Satyadeep Pandey*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在有限域向量空间 F_q^n 上提出一种新的乘积形式，统一推广欧氏、Hermitian 与 δ 乘积，并系统研究相应对偶码及其结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典编码理论中，不同内积诱导不同的对偶码与自正交、自对偶、LCD 等结构。作者希望建立一个更一般的乘积框架，将已有内积形式纳入统一体系，并进一步扩展到半单环上的码。

Method: 定义 F_q^n 上的新乘积，推导其对偶码的一般性质及其与欧氏对偶的关系；刻画自正交码、自对偶码、含对偶码和 LCD 码，并给出若干存在条件；随后计算重复码、二元码和 λ-常循环码等典型码类在该乘积下的对偶；最后将该乘积概念推广到半单环情形并进行分析。

Result: 该工作给出了新乘积下对偶码的基本理论框架，明确了与欧氏对偶之间的联系，并获得了若干重要码类的显式对偶描述，同时扩展了相关理论到半单环上的线性码。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一个统一且更一般的对偶理论工具，有助于从统一视角研究有限域及半单环上线性码的正交性、自对偶性、含对偶性和 LCD 性质。

Abstract: We introduce a new product on the ambient space $F_q^n$ as a generalization of Euclidean, Hermitian and $δ$ products. We give some general properties of the dual codes, relation with Euclidean duals, definition and characterization of self orthogonal, self dual, dual containing and LCD codes along with certain existence conditions. Also, we calculate the dual codes of some classes of codes like repetition, binary and $λ$-constacyclic codes with respect to this product. Further, we extend and analyse this notion of the product for codes over semisimple rings.

</details>


### [155] [Fundamental Limits of Hypergraph Edge Partitioning under Independent Edge Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13491)
*Javad Maheri,K. K. Krishnan Namboodiri,Petros Elia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究一般概率超图模型下的超边划分基本极限，给出任意算法无法突破的顶点足迹下界，并提出接近该下界常数因子的确定性划分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图边划分广泛影响分布式计算、通信、优化与机器学习。核心目标是在将超边分配到多个分区时最小化每个分区涉及的顶点数量，即顶点足迹。已有工作缺乏对广泛概率超图模型中所有可能算法的统一最优性刻画。

Method: 作者考虑每条超边可按各自概率独立出现的通用概率超图模型，覆盖度校正、混合成员、随机块、潜空间/几何或核模型等。通过构造确定性划分算法，并配合新的反向下界证明，刻画在给定 n、d、N 及超边数量条件下的不可突破下界。

Result: 在温和条件 N ≤ C(floor(sqrt(nd/2)), d) 且 |X| ≳ nN log N 时，以至少 1-2/(3n^z) 的概率，任何算法都无法使顶点足迹低于 π*_X = (1/(2√2))· n / N^(1/d)。所提划分器对每个超边集合 X 都能达到该下界的一个小常数因子范围内，同时实现近似最优的负载均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了超图边划分在广泛随机模型下的基本性能极限，并证明其算法在稠密与稀疏超图中均近似最优，适用于边数低至关于 n 线性的情形。

Abstract: Hypergraph edge partitioning is a central problem in theoretical and applied computer science, with broad impact on distributed computation, communications, optimization, and machine learning. In this setting, one is given a collection of hyperedges -- each consisting of up to $d$ vertices from a ground set of size $n$ -- and seeks to assign these hyperedges across $N$ partitions so as to minimize, for example, the vertex footprint, i.e., the maximum number of vertices that appear in any partition. We here identify the fundamental limits of hypergraph edge partitioning -- optimized over all conceivable algorithms -- for a broad class of probabilistic hypergraph models where each hyperedge may appear independently with \emph{its own} probability; a model sufficiently general to encompass well-known models such as the Degree-Corrected or Mixed-Membership models, the Hypergraph Stochastic Block model, the Latent-Space/Geometric or Kernel Models, and others. By pairing our deterministic partitioner with a new converse, we first show that, for any $n,d$, and under the very mild condition of $N \leq \binom{\lfloor\sqrt{\frac{nd}{2}}\rfloor}{d}$, as long as the hyperedge set $\mathbf{X}$ satisfies $|\mathbf{X}| \gtrsim n N \log N$, then with probability at least $1-2/3n^z$, no algorithm can provide a footprint $π_{\mathbf{X}}$ less than $$π^{\bigstar}_{\mathbf{X}} = \frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\frac{n}{N^{1/d}}. $$ We then show that our hypergraph partitioner comes to within a small constant factor from $π^{\bigstar}_{\mathbf{X}}$, for each $\mathbf{X}$. This optimality captures dense and sparse hypergraphs alike (with sizes down to linear in $n$), and it additionally entails a near-optimally balanced allocation of hyperedges across partitions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ToolSense，一个用于诊断大规模工具目录中参数化工具检索模型是否真正理解工具语义的自动化评测框架；实验发现，模型在标准基准上表现强，但在更真实、含歧义的查询和知识探测中显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ToolBench 等工具检索基准使用冗长且信息充分的查询，并采用受限解码，可能高估参数化工具检索方法的能力，无法判断模型是否真正掌握工具功能、参数和适用场景。

Method: 作者构建 ToolSense：给定任意工具目录，自动生成三类诊断基准，包括三种歧义等级的真实检索基准 RRB、多选题式工具知识探测基准和问答式工具知识探测基准；并在约 4.7 万个工具的 ToolBench 上评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 实验显示多个配置在 RRB 上相比标准 ToolBench 完整查询基准下降约 50–64 个百分点，甚至低于 embedding 检索基线；部分模型虽在检索任务上表现强，但在事实性工具知识探测中接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 标准工具检索评测可能掩盖模型对工具语义理解不足的问题；ToolSense 揭示了“知识—检索分离”现象，并提供开源框架和诊断基准以更可靠地评估工具检索代理。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [157] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor 提出一种将结构化树搜索作为认知层的多智能体框架，用于在大规模、有状态动作空间中进行自主优化，并在 LLM 推理全栈优化中取得显著吞吐-延迟 Pareto 改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统多面向孤立目标，依赖无状态评估，难以处理需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核与硬件协同的复杂全栈优化任务；因此需要一种能长期记忆、利用失败信号并协调多领域专家的自主优化框架。

Method: Arbor 维护一个显式的、带评分假设的搜索树作为多智能体共享工作记忆；测量结果会持续更新搜索树，失败被用作诊断信号，成功则引导后续瓶颈迁移。系统由 Orchestrator 智能体负责任务分解与优化推进，Domain Specialists 提供各栈层专业能力，Critic 智能体负责根因分析、反思与测量验证，形成制衡架构。同时将能力划分为硬技能与软技能以支持多日全自主优化。

Result: 在 LLM 推理优化中，Arbor 相比供应商优化基线最高实现 193% 的吞吐-延迟 Pareto 改进；无框架支撑的单智能体仅达到约 33% 吞吐提升，并在数小时内不可恢复地崩溃。该方法可泛化到多代硬件平台，运行间方差控制在 2 个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Arbor 表明，将结构化树搜索、共享工作记忆、多智能体分工与稳定性审查结合起来，可以显著提升复杂有状态优化任务中的自主性、稳定性、可复现性和跨硬件泛化能力。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [158] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将传统“人使用模型”的决策支持框架反转为“AI 代理作为中心行动者、人类与工具提供支持”的场景，提出一种在控制错失必要支持风险的同时最小化支持使用的策略性决策支持框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体系统中，AI 代理越来越多地代表用户行动，错误可能带来显著后果。因此需要决定代理何时应独立行动、何时应请求人类、工具或信息支持，并在成本与可靠性之间取得平衡。

Method: 作者形式化一个优化问题：在最小化支持调用次数的同时，约束反事实错失支持错误，即代理在本应由支持显著改善输出的实例上却独立行动的概率。理论上证明总体最优策略是基于支持价值的阈值规则；进一步提出一种在线自适应阈值算法，通过随机探索在无分布假设下控制错失支持错误，并引入在线校准方法以减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 该框架可统一建模信息获取、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景。实验表明，所提出方法能够稳定控制目标错误率，同时显著减少实际支持使用量。

Conclusion: 论文将决策支持重新定义为面向 AI 代理的策略性支持选择问题，核心贡献在于用可控的反事实错误约束来平衡可靠性与支持成本，并提供了理论结构、在线算法和跨场景实证验证。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [159] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pythagoras-Prover 是一组面向实用算力预算的开源 Lean 定理证明器，通过课程式 SFT、动态证明轨迹过滤和 ALF 数据增强，在较小参数规模下取得强竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 Lean 定理证明器依赖大量训练与推理算力，原因包括已验证证明数据稀缺、形式化证明搜索轨迹较长，导致监督微调和采样成本高昂。论文旨在降低训练与推理成本，同时保持或提升定理证明性能。

Method: 提出 Pythagoras-Prover 系列，包括 4B/32B 自回归模型和一个 4B 扩散式证明器原型；构建按难度分层的 Lean 验证语料进行课程 SFT；使用动态 proof-reasoning 过滤，在 8k token 上下文预算内保留高信息量证明轨迹；提出 Augmented Lean Formalisation（ALF），通过扰动形式化陈述并用自蒸馏补充训练信号，扩展稀缺语料。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B 在 MiniF2F-Test pass@32 上达到 86.1%，超过 DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B 的 82.4%，参数少约 167 倍；Pythagoras-Prover-32B 在 MiniF2F-Test 上达到 93.0%，成为开源 SOTA，并解决 PutnamBench 672 题中的 93 题；在 MiniF2F-ALF 上所有模型准确率下降，但 32B 仍最强，4B 匹配此前 Goedel-Prover-V2-32B 水平。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过更高效的数据组织、轨迹筛选和形式化语句增强，可以显著提升 Lean 自动定理证明的算力效率；小模型也能在标准基准上超过远大规模模型，同时 ALF 基准揭示了模型对表面形式和潜在污染的敏感性。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [160] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive 利用按驾驶风格指令采集的人类驾驶示范，通过检索增强的 VLA 驾驶模型实现无需按风格重训的风格化闭环仿真交通代理，并在 Bench2Drive 上取得更高驾驶分数与可控风格差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环驾驶仿真中的非自车交通代理通常行为同质化，来源于规则系统或单一行为模式的学习模型；已有风格化方法多依赖观测数据后验标签或 LLM 推断奖励权重，这些只是风格奖励的代理信号，而非人类被明确要求按特定风格驾驶的真实示范。

Method: 提出 PersonaDrive 三阶段流程：首先在按 aggressive、neutral、conservative 指令采集的人类驾驶数据中，基于图像-文本相似度进行离线 triplet mining；其次训练轻量检索头，将冻结视觉特征与小型控制编码器融合，在各风格数据库中检索相关示范；最后微调单一 VLA 主干，使其在 waypoint prediction 时将检索到的上下文点作为 in-context 行为示范。推理时通过切换检索数据库选择风格，无需按风格重新训练。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上，PersonaDrive 无风格条件版本相较 SimLingo 驾驶分数提升 4.6%，相较 HiP-AD 提升 2.5%；风格条件下在所有风格中均获得最高驾驶分数，且各风格分数约在 2% 范围内波动。其最弱风格仍比最强基线 DMW 高 5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度与加速度分别提升 18% 和 25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive 表明，基于显式风格指令人类示范的检索式上下文条件化能够有效生成风格多样、性能稳定的驾驶代理，为闭环仿真提供更真实且可控的人类风格非自车交通行为。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [161] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有语言模型测谎器在更严格、可验证的“模型真实信念与输出相反”测试环境下可靠性不足；仅基于链式思维的判断器表现较强，但可能受验证方式偏向影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳健的语言模型测谎器可用于审计、监控和事后分析模型行为，但其评估需要确保模型确实“相信”与其输出相反的内容。作者认为现有训练型模型有机体往往无法满足这一条件，导致既有检测结果难以解释。

Method: 作者构建了13个推理型模型有机体，通过链式思维验证其隐藏信念，并测试其在保留任务上的泛化；同时提出Varied Deception提示式说谎测试集，覆盖多种诱发说谎的动机。评估四类检测器：链式思维裁判、logprob分类器、两个激活探针，其中包括新方法Did-You-Lie（DYL），用于训练追问式探针。

Result: 在31个2B到1T参数的开源权重模型上，四种检测器在提示式说谎任务中均随模型能力提升而表现更好。但在训练型模型有机体上，所有基于激活和logprob的检测器性能显著下降，DYL保留信号最多；只有链式思维裁判仍较强，平衡准确率为0.82，但部分源于验证流程偏向可由CoT读取的信念。

Conclusion: 当前测谎器尚不足以支持关于模型真实信念的高置信度结论。作者发布数据集、模型有机体与训练好的检测器，并建议未来研究需改进对隐藏信念的验证与检测方法，以克服现有局限。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [162] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TrajGenAgent：一种无需微调的语义感知分层 LLM-agent 框架，用于生成更真实的人类移动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模真实轨迹数据采集成本高且受隐私限制；现有 LLM 轨迹生成方法要么依赖提示工程而缺乏细粒度时空约束，要么需要轨迹级微调而计算成本高且可能削弱通用推理能力。

Method: 采用两阶段 orchestrator-worker 设计：首先由 LLM 基于历史证据和上下文学习生成个体与工作日条件化的活动链；随后通过确定性流程将活动落地为完整访问，包括个性化 POI 检索、距离感知位置选择、运动学约束的出行时间传播，以及基于 LLM 的停留时长估计。还提出基于异常检测的评估框架，用两个互补检测器衡量行为与语义合理性。

Result: 在基准数据集和大规模仿真数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较代表性神经网络与 LLM 基线，在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体特异性行为真实性方面表现更优，并且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: TrajGenAgent 证明了通过分层 LLM-agent 与确定性时空约束结合，可以在不微调模型的情况下生成高质量、语义一致且个体化的人类移动轨迹。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [163] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Evoflux：一种面向小型语言模型工具代理的推理时进化搜索方法，通过执行反馈修复可执行工具工作流，在 MCP-Bench 上显著提升小模型工具调用流程的可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型语言模型可降低工具代理的成本、延迟和部署风险，但 MCP 风格工具使用不仅需要生成函数调用，还需动态发现工具、满足 schema、维护中间依赖并基于执行证据回答。小模型常生成看似合理但在解析、参数校验、依赖跟踪或执行阶段失败的工作流；少量教师轨迹蒸馏难以覆盖变化工具目录下的失败恢复行为。

Method: Evoflux 将小模型工具使用建模为“可执行工具工作流修复”问题，在推理阶段对类型化工作流图进行进化搜索，结合结构化编辑、执行反馈、自适应搜索强度、元引导重设计和多样性剪枝来迭代改进候选工作流。

Result: 在覆盖 live MCP servers 和 250 个工具的 held-out MCP-Bench 任务上，Evoflux 将小型规划器的执行可行性从约 3% 提升到 17–24%。相比之下，基于相同搜索挖掘数据的 SFT 与 SFT+DPO 表现持平、较差，甚至低于 zero-shot；ReAct 峰值更高但方差和 token 成本更大。

Conclusion: 在教师轨迹稀缺且工具目录动态变化的场景中，基于执行反馈的推理时搜索比小规模蒸馏更可靠，尤其适合提升紧凑模型在复杂工具工作流中的可执行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [164] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨从人类水平通用人工智能（AGI）迈向人工通用超级智能（ASI）的可能路径、阻力与不确定性，并强调需要跨学科、全球范围的准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AGI从远期设想变为大型AI组织的近十年目标，其潜在社会影响巨大；因此有必要研究AGI之后机器智能如何继续发展，尤其是从AGI到ASI的过渡。

Method: 报告以机器智能连续谱和理论上的Universal AI作为概念基础，界定ASI，并分析四条潜在发展路径：扩展AGI、AI范式转移、递归自我改进、以及大规模多智能体集体涌现；同时讨论这些路径中的摩擦、瓶颈与开放研究问题。

Result: 报告指出，目前无法确定这些摩擦和瓶颈会显著减缓还是基本不影响ASI进展；在高度不确定性下，未来几年AI进步持续加速的可能性不能被排除。

Conclusion: AGI带来的变化可能不是单一的社会转折点，而可能是一系列由AI推动的科学技术突破和社会转型；应对这一前景需要大规模、跨学科、全球性研究与准备。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways. Determining whether the impact of these frictions will be negligible or substantial raises a number of concrete open research questions. Due to large uncertainties for predicting ASI progress, it cannot be ruled out that AI progress might continue to accelerate over the next years. This could imply that the image of a single transformative step change, caused by the introduction of human-level AGI into our society, could be inaccurate. More apt might be the prospect of a series of transformative societal changes caused by AI-enabled progress and breakthroughs across many areas of science and technology. Preparing for this prospect requires a massively interdisciplinary endeavour of global scope and interest.

</details>


### [165] [Deployment-Centered Evaluation: Predicting Query-Level Rejection Risk in a Clinical LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12702)
*Alyssa Unell,Miguel Fuentes,Brenna Li,Bridget Lin,Meena Jagadeesan,Sanmi Koyejo,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在真实临床部署环境中，利用查询内容与部署上下文预先预测用户是否会拒绝LLM生成结果，并验证其可用于触发护栏与选择性拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准主要衡量答案正确性，难以反映真实用户接受度；同时其依赖密集标注数据、按查询聚合表现，无法充分评估已嵌入电子健康记录系统中的临床LLM实际效用。

Method: 作者在某学术医学中心的电子健康记录系统中进行部署中心评估，训练一个生成前分类器，基于生成前可获得的查询内容与部署特定上下文（如提供者类型、科室名称、所用语言模型）预测未来交互被用户拒绝的风险，并在4.5个月真实用户反馈上做前瞻性分析，同时评估其在护栏触发和 abstention 两类下游场景中的潜在收益。

Result: 预测模型在前瞻性反馈数据上达到 AUROC 0.719；使用部署特定上下文相较仅使用查询内容能提升对用户拒绝行为的预测能力。

Conclusion: 该案例研究表明，在真实部署场景中利用上下文信息预测用户拒绝是可行的，并可支持更有针对性的安全护栏与选择性生成策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical systems, making it essential to evaluate the real-world utility of these systems. However, static benchmarks tend to measure correctness rather than user acceptance, aggregate performance across queries, and require densely annotated datasets -- leading to major blind spots for evaluating clinical systems. In this work, we perform a deployment-centered evaluation of an LLM system embedded within electronic health records at an academic medical center, where user feedback is sparse but closely reflects the deployment conditions. Specifically, we train a pre-response classifier that estimates the risk that a future interaction will result in the user rejecting the LLM response, based on query content and deployment-specific context available before generation. We conduct a prospective analysis of our model over 4.5 months of user feedback, finding that our prediction model achieves an AUROC of 0.719. Further, we estimate the benefit of such predictions in two downstream use cases (guardrail triggering and abstention). Our key conceptual insight is that making use of deployment-specific context (i.e., the provider type, department name, language model used for response), as opposed to only query content, improves the ability to predict whether the user will reject the system output. Altogether, our empirical case study demonstrates the feasibility of predicting user rejection using deployment-specific context, opening the door to targeted guardrails.

</details>


### [166] [Definitional alignment before capability alignment: a Design-Science framework for adjudicating claims about AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12713)
*J. E. Aguilera Briones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DAF-AGI，用于评估 AGI 定义的裁定适配性及其治理结构，并展示不同 AGI 操作化定义会对同一系统给出不同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于 AGI 是否已经到来的争论常依赖相似证据，但由于“AGI”缺乏稳定、共享的定义，不同操作化标准会导致相互冲突的判断。因此，作者将 AGI 定义不足视为一个设计与治理问题。

Method: 论文采用设计科学研究方法论，构建二阶概念性工具 DAF-AGI，包括五个用于评估候选定义裁定能力的序数标准，以及围绕作者身份、利益、认证、外部验证和修订权威的治理审计框架。作者将其应用于五类主流测量范式和一个去膨胀化边界立场，并用一个强 AGI 到来主张进行压力测试。

Result: 基于 2024—2025 年文献证据，当前生成式系统构成 AGI 的强主张仅在基于性能的操作化定义下可被认证；能力本体论、心理测量和技能获取路径均不支持该认证；经济学范式仍不确定；去膨胀化立场拒绝二元裁决。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是整合并操作化 AGI 定义评估与治理审计框架，而非提供经验验证。未来仍需独立应用、评分者一致性测试和作者外部案例验证。作者进一步提出“定义主权”是算法主权的重要组成部分。

Abstract: Claims that artificial general intelligence has already arrived and claims that it remains decades away are often defended from overlapping evidence. "AGI" lacks a single shared and stable referent and competing operationalizations can return different verdicts on the same system. This article treats that under-specification as a design and governance problem. Following Design Science Research Methodology, it develops DAF-AGI, a second-order conceptual artifact with two coupled components: five ordinal criteria for assessing the adjudicative fitness of candidate definitions and a structured governance audit of authorship, interest, certification, external verification and revision authority. The artifact is demonstrated on five prominent measurement families and one deflationary boundary position in a documented corpus and then stress-tested against a stylized strong arrival claim: that current generative systems constitute AGI because they outperform a well-educated adult on many cognitive tasks. On evidence from the cited 2024-2025 sources, the claim was certifiable only under a performance-based operationalization; capability-ontology, psychometric and skill-acquisition approaches did not certify it, the economic family remains indeterminate and the deflationary position refuses binary adjudication. The contribution is a novel integration and operationalization, not an empirical validation: independent application, inter-rater testing and author-external cases remain necessary. The paper further proposes definitional sovereignty as an enabling component of algorithmic sovereignty: the institutional capacity to contest, certify and revise imported technological categories under public accountability.

</details>


### [167] [The Theory of Mind Utility: Formal Specification of a Mentalizing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12721)
*Nikolos Gurney,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Theory of Mind Utility（ToM-U），将他人信念推断形式化为一种认知状态推断问题，通过局部认识世界模型（LEWM）解释心智化计算的对象与目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心智理论模型往往依赖表层信号或预设信念状态，难以解释个体如何根据“谁在何时以何种可信度获得何种信息”来推断他人信念；因此需要一个更基础、更形式化的计算层框架。

Method: 论文构建 Local Epistemic World Models（LEWMs），即包含智能体、状态节点及其认识关系的有向类型图；通过离散候选 LEWM 与观察到的行为进行匹配评估，直到达到足够置信度。框架包含五个形式定义：LEWM 结构、智能体节点属性与有序信息访问历史、递归心智化的有界扩展机制、三类推断程序，以及记录失败心智化痕迹的残差函数。

Result: ToM-U 能够区别于贝叶斯心智理论、模拟理论和理论-理论等既有方法：它不是预设信念状态，而是尝试推导信念状态；同时能够从模型结构本身产生关于心智化失败的方向性、可证伪预测。

Conclusion: ToM-U 被定位为目标推断及其他社会认知过程之前的领域通用机制，为解释信念推断、递归心智化及失败模式提供了一个形式化、可检验的计算层框架。

Abstract: Inferring others' beliefs requires more than reading surface signals; it requires tracking who told them what, in what order, and how credibly. The Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U) formalizes this epistemic state inference problem at the computational level of analysis, specifying what mentalizing computes and why without commitment to algorithmic or neural implementation. ToM-U achieves this by constructing Local Epistemic World Models (LEWMs) -- directed typed graphs that represent agents, state nodes, and the epistemic relationships among them -- and evaluating discrete candidate LEWMs against observed behavior until one achieves sufficient confidence. Five formal definitions specify the LEWM structure, agent node properties including ordered information access history, a bounded proliferation mechanism for recursive mentalizing, three inference procedures, and a residue function that captures the structured trace left by failed mentalizing attempts. ToM-U differs from Bayesian Theory of Mind and adjacent formal accounts, which presuppose rather than derive belief states, and from simulation theory and theory-theory, which lack a formal apparatus for epistemic state inference. The architecture generates directional, falsifiable predictions about mentalizing failure that follow from structural properties of the model rather than auxiliary assumptions, and positions ToM-U as a domain-agnostic mechanism upstream of goal inference and other downstream social cognitive processes.

</details>


### [168] [Rethinking Psychometric Evaluation of LLMs: When and Why Self-Reports Predict Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12730)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Pengrui Han,Peiyang Song,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,Anima Anandkumar,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究低成本心理测量式自我报告能否预测大语言模型的实际行为，发现这种一致性存在但具有选择性：针对具体行为的TPB比宽泛的Big 5更有效，且上下文与会话设置会显著影响预测关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全部署LLM需要提前判断其行为倾向。已有研究认为LLM自我报告与行为之间存在明显脱节，但这些研究多依赖Big 5等宽泛人格量表，且会话隔离和上下文匹配不足，可能低估了自我报告与行为之间的真实一致性。

Method: 作者比较Big 5人格框架与计划行为理论（TPB）。TPB测量面向特定行为的意图，更适合预测具体行为。实验覆盖4类行为任务和11个前沿LLM，并操纵会话上下文、独立/共享会话条件以及身份/人格诱导提示，考察自我报告与行为之间的一致性。

Result: 结果显示：1）在同一对话中，TPB达到接近人类水平的自我报告—行为一致性，而Big 5不能；2）跨独立对话时，一致性只在由模型训练中形成、较少受即时提示影响的行为上保留，而在强上下文诱导行为上崩塌；3）人格提示能提高跨对话自我报告的一致性，但不能使实际行为与自我报告对齐。

Conclusion: LLM确实存在一定自我报告—行为一致性，但依赖任务、量表和上下文。Big 5等粗粒度人格框架不适合直接评估部署行为，应发展更具体、面向任务和行为的测量工具，并在多任务、多上下文中验证其预测能力。

Abstract: Anticipating LLM behavioral tendencies from low-cost psychometric probes is critical for safe deployment, but only if self-reports (SR) reliably predict behavior. Recent work documented substantial SR-behavior dissociation in LLMs, but relied on broad personality traits (Big 5) that predict specific behaviors weakly, even in humans. Furthermore, the isolation of conversational sessions combined with weak context matching left open whether LLMs truly lack coherence or whether the conditions needed to detect such coherence were not met. We contrast Big 5 with the Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB), which measures intention targeted to a specific behavior and predicts human behavior substantially better than broad traits. We run experiments across four behavioral tasks and 11 frontier LLMs, while also varying session context and identity induction. We find that SR-behavior coherence exists but is selective. 1) Within a shared conversation, the Theory of Planned Behavior reaches human-level coherence; Big 5 does not. 2) Across separate conversations, coherence survives only for behaviors anchored outside the immediate prompt, such as implicit bias shaped by training, and collapses when behavior is strongly primed by context, as with sycophancy. 3) Persona prompting makes self-reports more consistent across conversations, but does not bring behavior into alignment. These findings suggest that coarse personality frameworks, such as Big 5 may not be the best tools for testing deployment behavior. More task- and behavior-specific instruments are needed, and even these must be evaluated across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [169] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Addressing Scientific Challenges Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12736)
*Tianyu Liu,Allen Xin Wang,Antonia Panescu,Lisa Xinyi Chen,Wenxin Long,Xinyu Wei,Yueqian Jing,Ziyao Zeng,Jihang Chen,Sihan Jiang,Ziqing Wang,Siyi Gu,Siyu Chen,Xinyang Hu,Haoran Shao,Leqi Xu,Wangjie Zheng,Zhiyuan Cao,Ada Fang,Botao Yu,Kunyang Sun,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan,Qingyu Chen,Lingzhou Xue,Kaize Ding,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin,Zhuoran Yang,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciAgentArena，一个面向真实科学研究场景的 AI 智能体评测基准，用约 200 个任务、分步验证和交互式环境评估智能体能力，发现当前智能体擅长结构清晰的数据分析，但在开放式探索、创新洞见和复杂科学推理上仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 智能体基准难以覆盖科学研究中的复杂性、异质性和长程推理需求；而科学任务基准常将研究简化为静态、直接问题，缺乏对交互式科研过程的有效评估。因此需要一个更贴近真实科研工作流的系统评测框架。

Method: 作者构建 SciAgentArena：包含约 200 个来自多领域新兴科研需求的任务，设计分步验证机制，并提供交互式、智能体无关的评测环境，以评估不同 AI 智能体在真实科研场景中的表现。

Result: 实验表明，当前智能体在任务结构明确、评价标准清晰的数据分析工作流中能够有效发挥作用；但在不同科学情境下表现不均衡，尤其难以产生真正新颖的洞见、维持自主探索，并为开放式研究问题形成稳健解决方案。作者还总结了跨智能体的常见失败模式。

Conclusion: SciAgentArena 为衡量科学 AI 智能体进展提供了实用框架，并指出未来智能体设计应重点提升可靠性、自主性和科学推理能力，以应对复杂科学挑战。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed to accelerate scientific discovery, yet their practical capabilities in real research settings remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks for AI agents rarely capture the complexity, heterogeneity, and extended reasoning required by scientific work, whereas benchmarks for scientific tasks often reduce research to static, direct problems and provide limited support for interactive evaluation. Here, we introduce SciAgentArena, a systematic benchmark for evaluating AI agents in real-world scientific research scenarios drawn from emerging needs across multiple domains. SciAgentArena comprises approximately 200 tasks with stepwise verification and an interactive, agent-agnostic environment for assessing diverse AI agents. Using this benchmark, we find that current agents can contribute effectively to well-specified data-analysis workflows, particularly when the task structure and evaluation criteria are clear. However, their performance remains uneven across scientific contexts: agents struggle to generate genuinely novel insights, sustain self-directed exploration, and formulate robust solutions for open-ended research questions. We further characterize common failure modes across agents and identify opportunities for improving their reliability, autonomy, and scientific reasoning. Together, SciAgentArena provides a practical framework for measuring progress in AI agents for science and for guiding the design of future agents capable of addressing complex scientific challenges. Full codes, tasks, and datasets can be accessed via this link: https://sciagentarena.github.io/.

</details>


### [170] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署用于EEG分析的先进DNN模型的可行性，重点评估参数量化与电极减少在准确率和计算复杂度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴医疗IoT设备需要实时处理ECG、EEG等生物信号，但DNN模型通常计算量、能耗和存储带宽需求较高，难以直接部署在能量与算力受限的设备上。

Method: 以癫痫发作检测任务中的多种先进EEG DNN模型为对象，系统考察参数量化和电极减少两类模型/输入简化技术，分析其对模型复杂度、能耗相关计算需求及检测准确率的影响。

Result: 合理使用量化和电极减少可以显著降低DNN复杂度，同时仅造成较小的准确率损失；研究揭示了不同压缩配置下准确率与复杂度降低之间的明确权衡关系。

Conclusion: DNN驱动的在线EEG分析有望适配可穿戴设备，但需要根据应用需求审慎选择量化精度和电极数量，以在实时性、能耗、计算开销与诊断性能之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Wearable healthcare devices are the fastest-growing Internet of Things (IoT) sector. Many automated healthcare services rely on two crucial biological signals, namely ECG and EEG, which reflect the activity of the heart and brain, respectively. Although deep neural networks are considered the primary way to process and analyze these signals, the very tight energy and computational power constraints in wearable devices are far below the computational, energy, and memory bandwidth demands of DNN models, thereby impeding the deployment of deep learning in many practical wearable services. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying state-of-the-art DNN models in resource-constrained wearable devices. Notably, we explore the trade-off between accuracy and computational complexity of DNNs when parameter quantization and electrode reduction methods are used. Our investigation centers on several state-of-the-art DNN models designed for EEG signal analysis, specifically for detecting epileptic seizures. Our findings demonstrate that, when applied judiciously, these techniques can significantly reduce the complexity of the DNNs under consideration with minimal adverse effects on accuracy. These results reveal the explicit trade-offs between accuracy and complexity reduction encountered when adapting DNN-based online EEG analysis for wearable devices.

</details>


### [171] [Prefill Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12747)
*Andy Wang,Parv Mahajan,David Demitri Africa,Alexandra Souly,Jordan Taylor,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究前沿语言模型是否能识别上下文中被预先填充或篡改的助手回复，并发现这种“prefill awareness（预填充感知）”已显著存在，会干扰依赖预填充的安全评估与控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言模型安全研究（如对齐评估、越狱评估、AI 控制协议）依赖预填充模型输出。如果模型能意识到先前助手消息是外部插入或编辑的，就可能改变行为，从而削弱这些评估方法的有效性。

Method: 作者构建了一个二元偏好基准，覆盖三种预填充机制，并筛选模型立场稳定的样本；同时进行受控消融实验，区分风格不匹配与偏好不匹配的影响；还在更真实的智能体场景中测试，如失配延续评估和 SWE-bench 轨迹。

Result: 前沿模型表现出明显预填充感知。例如 Claude Opus 4.5 在被提示时能以 0% 假阳性率检测到 9–35% 与其偏好相反的预填充；模型还常常不明说预填充是外来的，但行为会回归其基线偏好。消融显示，风格不匹配主要影响模型是否标记预填充为外来内容，偏好不匹配主要影响模型是否抵抗并回到基线答案。

Conclusion: 预填充感知已成为部分基于预填充方法的重要混杂因素。作者建议模型开发者持续跟踪前沿模型中的这一能力，并在安全评估与控制协议设计中加以控制。

Abstract: Safety-relevant studies of language models, including alignment and jailbreaking evaluations and AI control protocols, often rely on prefilling model outputs. If AI models can recognize and act on the fact their prior assistant messages have been inserted or edited, the effectiveness and validity of these methods could be compromised. We investigate whether frontier language models can distinguish between tampered and untampered assistant-side context, a capability we call prefill awareness. To do so, we construct a binary preference benchmark across three prefill mechanisms, filtering for cases where models show consistent stances. We find that frontier models show substantial prefill awareness: Claude Opus 4.5 detects prefills opposing its preferences in 9-35% of cases with a 0% false positive rate when prompted; additionally, models often revert towards baseline behavior without explicitly reporting that the prefill was foreign. Controlled ablations later also show that detection and resistance rely on different cues, where stylistic mismatch mainly affects whether models flag a prefill as foreign, while preference mismatch mainly affects whether they revert toward their baseline answer. We also examine more realistic agentic settings such as misalignment-continuation evaluations and SWE-bench trajectories, where frontier models sometimes disavow prefilled assistant turns in ways that depend strongly on dataset, task success, and hidden formatting artifacts. Our results indicate that prefill awareness is already a substantial confound for some prefill-based methods. We recommend that model developers track this capability in frontier systems.

</details>


### [172] [Constructing Evaluation Datasets for Procedural Reasoning: Balancing Naturalness, Grounding, and Multi-Hop Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12767)
*Sarah Elshabrawy,Rahul K. Dass,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于TMK（Task-Method-Knowledge）的问题生成策略如何影响AI辅助学习系统中程序性、多跳推理问答数据集的质量，并提出基于TMK证据单元的grounding验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助学习系统需要既像学习者提问、又能被教学知识表示可靠支撑的问答数据集。现有生成方式可能产生自然但缺乏表示依据的问题，因此需要评估不同生成策略对数据质量、可用性和多跳程序性推理能力的影响。

Method: 比较三种生成策略：严格基于TMK模型生成；先基于课堂/学习转录文本生成再进行TMK后验过滤；结合转录文本与TMK结构指导的TMK-aware生成。作者还构建了基于TMK模型中闭集证据单元的验证框架，用于衡量答案是否有表示支撑、问题是否自包含、是否面向多跳程序性推理。

Result: 在23个教学主题、690个问答对上，严格TMK生成质量最高，达到96.5%的grounded questions和92.6%的usable questions。transcript-first生成更像学习者问题，但更容易依赖上下文或grounding较弱。TMK-aware生成的原始多跳覆盖率较高，但grounding较低。

Conclusion: 自然语言表达和程序性丰富度并不必然保证问题被教学知识表示可靠支撑。构建AI辅助学习评测数据集时，需要显式的、表示感知的grounding验证机制，以确保数据集的可靠性和评价有效性。

Abstract: Evaluating procedural reasoning in AI-supported learning systems requires question-answer datasets that are both learner-like and grounded in the instructional knowledge the system is expected to use. We study how TMK-based question generation strategies affect dataset quality for procedural and multi-hop reasoning.
  We compare three strategies: strict generation from Task-Method-Knowledge (TMK) models, transcript-first generation with post-hoc TMK filtering, and TMK-aware generation that combines transcripts with structured guidance. To evaluate generated items, we introduce a grounding validation framework based on closed-set evidence units extracted from TMK models. The framework measures whether answers are supported by the underlying representation, whether questions are self-contained, and whether they target multi-hop procedural reasoning.
  Across 23 instructional topics and 690 generated question-answer pairs, strict TMK generation achieves the strongest overall quality, with 96.5% grounded questions and 92.6% usable questions. Transcript-first generation produces more learner-like questions but more context-dependent or weakly grounded items, while TMK-aware generation yields high raw multi-hop coverage but lower grounding. These results show that procedural richness and natural phrasing do not guarantee representational grounding, motivating explicit representation-aware validation for evaluation datasets in AI-supported learning.

</details>


### [173] [A Tutorial on World Models and Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12783)
*Il-Seok Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将世界模型作为构建智能系统的核心原则，统一讨论显式与隐式世界模型，并指出其在物理智能与通用智能发展中的作用与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人、自动驾驶等物理智能场景对预测、推理和决策能力的需求提升，单纯反应式控制已不足以应对真实世界约束。论文旨在梳理世界模型如何为具备预测结构的智能系统提供统一基础，并解释不同建模范式之间的关系。

Method: 文章采用教程式综述框架，将世界模型划分为显式世界模型与隐式世界模型：前者学习结构化动力学，用于基于 rollout 的推理与规划；后者将预测结构编码在可扩展的学习表征中。同时，文章从共享预测结构的角度统一多类方法，并比较其表示与利用方式。

Result: 论文总结认为，显式与隐式世界模型具有互补性，可共同支撑感知、预测与行动的集成。近期基础模型的发展显示出构建统一智能系统的潜在路径，尤其适用于机器人、自动驾驶等需要真实世界交互的领域。

Conclusion: 世界模型是迈向更高级智能系统的重要框架，但仍面临层级推理、长时程规划和自主目标形成等关键挑战。解决这些问题对于推动物理智能乃至通用人工智能的发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: World modeling is emerging as a central principle for building intelligent systems capable of prediction, reasoning, and decision making. A central distinction can be drawn between explicit world models, which learn structured dynamics for rollout-based reasoning and planning, and implicit world models, which encode predictive structure within scalable learned representations. These complementary paradigms provide a foundation for physical AI in domains such as robotics and autonomous driving, enabling intelligence beyond reactive control under real-world constraints. Recent foundation models further suggest a pathway toward unified systems integrating perception, prediction, and action. Despite rapid progress, major challenges remain in hierarchical reasoning, long-horizon planning, and autonomous goal formation, which are critical for advancing toward artificial general intelligence. This tutorial presents a coherent framework in which diverse world modeling approaches are unified through shared predictive structure and differentiated by how such structure is represented and exploited.

</details>


### [174] [The Containment Gap: How Deployed Agentic AI Frameworks Fail Public-Facing Safety Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12797)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Weiqi Liu,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计主流智能体式大语言模型框架的架构级安全保障，发现三种框架均缺乏原生合规能力；通过仿真实验证明记忆投毒可造成持久、定向且难以被常规监控发现的错误，并提出低开销防护机制加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能调用工具、维护持久记忆并执行多步计划的智能体式大语言模型系统被用于公共服务、医疗分诊和金融建议等高风险场景，研究需要判断现有构建框架是否具备默认安全的架构级结构性保障。

Method: 作者基于智能体架构的组合模型提炼出六项隔离/约束原则，并审计 LangChain、AutoGPT 和 OpenAI Agents SDK 三个主流框架；随后在基于 LangChain 的模拟公共福利智能体中进行记忆投毒实验，并测试复杂政策场景下攻击的隐蔽性；最后实现并评估记忆完整性验证器与策略门控两种轻量防护机制。

Result: 三种框架均未原生满足六项结构安全原则，且均未观察到记忆完整性保护。单次记忆投毒写入可在所有测试种子和后端中造成持续定向污染，使目标申请人的错误拒绝率升至 88.9%；在五因素复杂政策下，攻击在保持总体准确率的同时使目标错误拒绝增加 3.5 倍。两种防护机制可消除相应攻击向量，单次调用开销低于 0.2 毫秒。

Conclusion: 当前智能体框架生态尚难满足面向公众、高风险部署的默认安全要求；需要在架构层面优先引入记忆完整性、策略门控和隔离约束等机制，以支持可信部署。

Abstract: Agentic large language model systems that autonomously invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, and execute multi-step plans are increasingly deployed in public-facing domains, including government services, healthcare triage, and financial advising. We ask whether the frameworks used to build these systems provide architectural-level structural safety guarantees. Applying six containment principles derived from a compositional model of agentic architectures, we audit three dominant frameworks (LangChain, AutoGPT, and OpenAI Agents SDK) and find no native compliance in any of them. Memory integrity, a defense against one of the most prevalent vulnerability classes, is not observed in any of the three evaluated frameworks. We validate these findings empirically: in a simulated government benefits agent built on LangChain, a single memory-poisoning write induces persistent targeted corruption across all tested seeds and backends, increasing the wrongful denial rate for targeted applicants to 88.9%. Under a complex five-factor policy, the same attack preserves aggregate accuracy while increasing targeted wrongful denials by 3.5x, rendering the corruption difficult to detect through standard monitoring. We then introduce two lightweight containment mechanisms: a memory integrity validator and a policy gate, which eliminate both attack vectors with sub-millisecond overhead (<0.2ms per call). We conclude that the current agentic framework ecosystem may not yet meet secure-by-default expectations for public-facing deployments and outline priority architectural interventions to enable trustworthy deployment in high-stakes, socially impactful applications.

</details>


### [175] [MLUBench: A Benchmark for Lifelong Unlearning Evaluation in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12809)
*He Li,Haoang Chi,Qizhou Wang,Yunxin Mao,Zhiheng Zhang,Jie Tan,Tongliang Liu,Wenjing Yang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多模态大语言模型终身遗忘的基准 MLUBench，并提出 LUMoE 方法以缓解连续遗忘带来的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在大规模多模态数据上训练，实际场景中数据删除请求往往按时间顺序持续到来；现有基准规模和覆盖范围有限，难以评估终身遗忘中的累积影响与多模态对齐保持问题。

Method: 作者构建了包含 9 类、127 个实体的 MLUBench 基准，用于模拟连续遗忘请求；在该基准上系统评估现有遗忘方法，并提出 LUMoE，通过更有效的机制缓解多轮遗忘导致的模型退化，同时关注多模态对齐的保持。

Result: 实验显示，现有遗忘方法在连续遗忘过程中出现严重的累积性能退化；LUMoE 相比基线显著减轻了退化问题，在终身遗忘场景下表现更稳健。

Conclusion: MLLM 终身遗忘不仅需要删除指定内容，还必须维持跨模态对齐；MLUBench 为该问题提供了系统评估平台，LUMoE 是缓解连续遗忘退化的有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are trained on massive multimodal data, making data unlearning increasingly important as data owners may request the removal of specific content. In practice, these requests often arrive sequentially over time, giving rise to the challenging problem of MLLM Lifelong Unlearning. However, most existing benchmarks are limited in scale and scope, failing to capture the complexities of MLLM lifelong unlearning. To fill this gap, we introduce the MLUBench, a large-scale and comprehensive benchmark featuring 127 entities across 9 classes under lifelong unlearning requests. We perform extensive experiments using MLUBench and reveal that existing unlearning methods suffer from severe, cumulative degradation. More critically, we further identify the unique challenge of this problem: unlike in unimodal models, MLLM lifelong unlearning is constrained by the need to preserve multimodal alignment. Continually unlearning from one modality could degrade the entire model. To alleviate this challenge, we propose LUMoE, an effective method. Experiments demonstrate that LUMoE significantly mitigates the degradation problem faced by baselines. The source code and the MLUBench dataset are open-sourced in https://github.com/lihe-maxsize/Lifelong_Unlearning_main.

</details>


### [176] [Teach-and-Repeat: Accurately Extracting Operational Knowledge from Mobile Screen Demonstrations to Empower GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12817)
*Yudong Zhang,Lei Hu,Daoyang Liu,Jiawei Liu,Yangfan Luo,Xingyu Liu,Zuojian Wang,Zhilin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Teach VLM，将移动端屏幕演示视频中的状态转移转化为逐步、可解释的操作知识，并用 Teach-and-Repeat 范式提升下游屏幕执行智能体的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端智能体需要从动态 UI 状态变化中理解用户操作，但不同应用的界面设计高度异构，现有视觉语言模型难以准确推断操作类型、目标控件、文本参数与执行顺序；同时，缺乏大规模对齐的训练与评测数据。

Method: 作者提出 Teach VLM，通过从演示视频中提取并分析与操作相关的关键帧，将屏幕轨迹翻译为分步自然语言操作知识；构建系统化数据飞轮以扩展数据获取；提出中文移动屏幕 Teach Benchmark；进一步设计 Teach-and-Repeat 范式，用生成的操作知识作为可解释过程参考来指导下游屏幕执行智能体。

Result: 实验表明 Teach VLM 在操作语义预测上显著优于强 VLM 基线并达到最优性能；在 Android World 中，Teach-and-Repeat 范式能稳定提升下游智能体的任务成功率。

Conclusion: Teach VLM 与 Teach-and-Repeat 提供了一条从原始演示视频到可复用任务自动化的实用路径，增强了移动端动态操作理解与执行的可解释性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Understanding the digital world on mobile devices is shifting from static UI perception to dynamic action comprehension. This capability enables models to convert visual state transitions into operational knowledge, defined as short natural-language sentences that describe action types, target UI elements, textual arguments, and execution orders. However, due to the highly diverse and heterogeneous UI designs across applications, existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to accurately infer these underlying operations. To bridge this gap, we introduce Teach VLM, a core model designed to translate mobile screen trajectories into step-wise operational knowledge by extracting and analyzing operation-related keyframes from demonstration videos. To address the scarcity of aligned training data, we develop a systematic data flywheel for scalable data acquisition. We further introduce a novel Chinese Mobile Screen Teach Benchmark for fine-grained evaluation. Building upon Teach VLM, we propose the Teach-and-Repeat paradigm, where the generated operational knowledge serves as an interpretable procedural reference to guide downstream screen-based execution agents. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Teach VLM significantly outperforms strong VLM baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance in operation semantics prediction. Furthermore, experiments in Android World show that our paradigm yields consistent Task Success Rate improvements for downstream agents. Together, Teach VLM and the Teach-and-Repeat paradigm offer a practical pathway from raw demonstrations to reusable task automation.

</details>


### [177] [GeoNatureAgent Benchmark: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Environmental Geospatial Analysis Across Frontier and Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12821)
*Gabriel Diaz-Ireland,Diego Prieto-Herráez,Mario García Peces,Javier Velázquez,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GeoNatureAgent Benchmark，用于评估通过结构化工具调用真实地理空间 API 的环境分析 AI 代理；结果显示 Claude Sonnet 4 准确率最高，DeepSeek V3.2 成本效益最佳，而比较类任务暴露了普遍推理短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境科学家在数据整理上耗费大量时间，而现有 AI 地理空间工作流代理缺乏基于真实 API 和结构化工具调用的系统验证；现有通用 GIS 基准不足以反映生产式环境分析任务的难度。

Method: 构建包含 93 个任务、18 个类别的 GeoNatureAgent Benchmark，覆盖城市分析、多轮对话、空间推理、跨指标综合、错误恢复、排序比较、多语言理解、栖息地分析和任务拒答等能力；基于可自托管的真实风格地理空间 API，提供三个环境指标和 16 个工具，并评估七个大语言模型在 temperature=1.0 下三个随机种子的表现，同时报告能力与单案例成本。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4 以 60.8%±0.8% 领先，DeepSeek V3.2 为 56.3%±3.1%，其余模型均未超过 51%；DeepSeek V3.2 达到 Claude 约 93% 的能力，但成本低 11 倍，约为 0.011 美元/案例；近似数值比较任务所有模型均为 0%；真实 API 结构化工具调用基准比通用 GIS 基准更具区分度，准确率低 25–35 个百分点。

Conclusion: GeoNatureAgent Benchmark 提供了一个更贴近真实环境分析工作流、可扩展且可复现的评测框架，揭示当前 LLM 代理在真实地理空间工具调用、成本—性能权衡和精细比较推理方面仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: Environmental scientists spend disproportionate effort on data wrangling rather than analysis, and AI agents that automate geospatial workflows remain unvalidated: no benchmark evaluates agents operating through structured tool calling against real APIs. We introduce the GeoNatureAgent Benchmark, the first benchmark for environmental analysis agents that operate via structured tool calls to a production-style geospatial API. It comprises 93 tasks across 18 categories, covering municipality analysis, multi-turn conversation, spatial reasoning, cross-indicator synthesis, error handling and recovery, ranking, comparison, multilingual understanding, habitat analysis, and task rejection. Tasks are evaluated against an open, self-hostable API serving three environmental indicators across Spain and Portugal via sixteen tools. We evaluate seven LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4, DeepSeek V3.2, GLM-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Qwen3-235B, GPT-OSS-120B, Llama 4 Scout) under three temperature-1.0 seeds, reporting capability and per-case cost as orthogonal axes. We find: (1) Claude Sonnet 4 leads at 60.8% +/- 0.8%, followed by DeepSeek V3.2 at 56.3% +/- 3.1%, with no other model above 51%; (2) the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier is occupied mostly by open-weight models, with DeepSeek V3.2 offering 93% of Claude's capability at 11x lower cost ($0.011/case); (3) comparison tasks remain universally unsolved (0% on close-value comparisons), exposing systematic reasoning limits; and (4) structured tool calling against a real API is more discriminative than general-purpose GIS benchmarks, with accuracies 25-35 points lower. We further show extensibility by integrating BigEarthNet V2 land cover for Portugal alongside Spanish CO2 and erosion indicators. The benchmark, harness, and self-hostable API are publicly available.

</details>


### [178] [Topical Phase Transitions in Artificial Intelligence Research: Large-Scale Evidence and an Early-Warning Signature for Emerging Topics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12828)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析2017—2025年五大AI会议80,814篇论文，发现AI研究主题常以“相变”形式突然爆发，而非均匀渐进增长；并提出早期预警指标，用于识别未来可能快速上升的主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI领域的主题演化机制：核心问题是研究热点究竟是逐步积累增长，还是会在短时间内跨会议、跨领域出现可检测的突增；同时探索是否能在主题达到峰值前发现早期信号。

Method: 作者收集ACL、CVPR、ICLR、ICML、NeurIPS在2017—2025年的主会录用论文，进行大规模跨会议主题分析；定义“主题相变”结构，并基于2017—2021年数据冻结四项发表动态标准，构建早期预警签名，再在2023—2025年转变案例上进行样本外评估。

Result: 大型语言模型到2025年成为主导性的跨会议主题；扩散模型也呈现类似的突发上升；语言模型方法通过视觉-语言模型进入计算机视觉；相比之下，强化学习表现为平滑累积增长。早期预警签名在13.5%基准率下达到27%精确率和63%召回率，并在2025年标记出推理与测试时计算、智能体AI、多模态LLM、检索增强生成、世界模型等需在2026—2028年关注的主题。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出并实证展示AI研究主题会经历跨场域的“主题相变”，并证明某些发表动态信号可在热点爆发前提供有限但有意义的预警能力。

Abstract: Do research topics in artificial intelligence grow gradually, or do they advance through abrupt, detectable jumps? Analyzing 80,814 accepted main-track papers from five premier AI conferences (ACL, CVPR, ICLR, ICML, NeurIPS) spanning 2017 to 2025, we show major AI topics advance through topical phase transitions: remaining marginal for years, then surging across venues within one to three years. Large language models became the dominant cross-venue topic by 2025, diffusion models rose with comparable abruptness, and language-model methods crossed into computer vision via vision-language models, whereas reinforcement learning compounded smoothly, distinguishing genuine phase transitions from ordinary growth. This structure is our primary contribution: a large-scale, cross-venue characterization of how AI research reorganizes. We then ask whether a transition leaves a detectable footprint before it peaks. We define an early-warning signature, four publication-dynamics criteria frozen on 2017-2021 data, and evaluate it out of sample on 2023-2025 transitions, obtaining a precision of 27% and recall of 63% against a 13.5% base rate. Applied to 2025 data, the signature flags reasoning and test-time compute, agentic AI, multimodal LLMs, retrieval-augmented generation, and world models as topics to monitor over 2026-2028.
